It handles both closed (unitary) and open (dissipative) quantum systems with multiple solvers optimized for different scenarios.\n\n## Installation\n\n```bash\nuv pip install qutip\n```\n\nOptional packages for additional functionality:\n\n```bash\n# Quantum information processing (circuits, gates)\nuv pip install qutip-qip\n\n# Quantum trajectory viewer\nuv pip install qutip-qtrl\n```\n\n## Quick Start\n\n```python\nfrom qutip import *\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n# Create quantum state\npsi = basis(2, 0)  # |0⟩ state\n\n# Create operator\nH = sigmaz()  # Hamiltonian\n\n# Time evolution\ntlist = np.linspace(0, 10, 100)\nresult = sesolve(H, psi, tlist, e_ops=[sigmaz()])\n\n# Plot results\nplt.plot(tlist, result.expect[0])\nplt.xlabel('Time')\nplt.ylabel('⟨σz⟩')\nplt.show()\n```\n\n## Core Capabilities\n\n### 1. Quantum Objects and States\n\nCreate and manipulate quantum states and operators:\n\n```python\n# States\npsi = basis(N, n)  # Fock state |n⟩\npsi = coherent(N, alpha)  # Coherent state |α⟩\nrho = thermal_dm(N, n_avg)  # Thermal density matrix\n\n# Operators\na = destroy(N)  # Annihilation operator\nH = num(N)  # Number operator\nsx, sy, sz = sigmax(), sigmay(), sigmaz()  # Pauli matrices\n\n# Composite systems\npsi_AB = tensor(psi_A, psi_B)  # Tensor product\n```\n\n**See** `references/core_concepts.md` for comprehensive coverage of quantum objects, states, operators, and tensor products.\n\n### 2. Time Evolution and Dynamics\n\nMultiple solvers for different scenarios:\n\n```python\n# Closed systems (unitary evolution)\nresult = sesolve(H, psi0, tlist, e_ops=[num(N)])\n\n# Open systems (dissipation)\nc_ops = [np.sqrt(0.1) * destroy(N)]  # Collapse operators\nresult = mesolve(H, psi0, tlist, c_ops, e_ops=[num(N)])\n\n# Quantum trajectories (Monte Carlo)\nresult = mcsolve(H, psi0, tlist, c_ops, ntraj=500, e_ops=[num(N)])\n```\n\n**Solver selection guide:**\n- `sesolve`: Pure states, unitary evolution\n- `mesolve`: Mixed states, dissipation, general open systems\n- `mcsolve`: Quantum jumps, photon counting, individual trajectories\n- `brmesolve`: Weak system-bath coupling\n- `fmmesolve`: Time-periodic Hamiltonians (Floquet)\n\n**See** `references/time_evolution.md` for detailed solver documentation, time-dependent Hamiltonians, and advanced options.\n\n### 3. Analysis and Measurement\n\nCompute physical quantities:\n\n```python\n# Expectation values\nn_avg = expect(num(N), psi)\n\n# Entropy measures\nS = entropy_vn(rho)  # Von Neumann entropy\nC = concurrence(rho)  # Entanglement (two qubits)\n\n# Fidelity and distance\nF = fidelity(psi1, psi2)\nD = tracedist(rho1, rho2)\n\n# Correlation functions\ncorr = correlation_2op_1t(H, rho0, taulist, c_ops, A, B)\nw, S = spectrum_correlation_fft(taulist, corr)\n\n# Steady states\nrho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n```\n\n**See** `references/analysis.md` for entropy, fidelity, measurements, correlation functions, and steady state calculations.\n\n### 4. Visualization\n\nVisualize quantum states and dynamics:\n\n```python\n# Bloch sphere\nb = Bloch()\nb.add_states(psi)\nb.show()\n\n# Wigner function (phase space)\nxvec = np.linspace(-5, 5, 200)\nW = wigner(psi, xvec, xvec)\nplt.contourf(xvec, xvec, W, 100, cmap='RdBu')\n\n# Fock distribution\nplot_fock_distribution(psi)\n\n# Matrix visualization\nhinton(rho)  # Hinton diagram\nmatrix_histogram(H.full())  # 3D bars\n```\n\n**See** `references/visualization.md` for Bloch sphere animations, Wigner functions, Q-functions, and matrix visualizations.\n\n### 5. Advanced Methods\n\nSpecialized techniques for complex scenarios:\n\n```python\n# Floquet theory (periodic Hamiltonians)\nT = 2 * np.pi / w_drive\nf_modes, f_energies = floquet_modes(H, T, args)\nresult = fmmesolve(H, psi0, tlist, c_ops, T=T, args=args)\n\n# HEOM (non-Markovian, strong coupling)\nfrom qutip.nonmarkov.heom import HEOMSolver, BosonicBath\nbath = BosonicBath(Q, ck_real, vk_real)\nhsolver = HEOMSolver(H_sys, [bath], max_depth=5)\nresult = hsolver.run(rho0, tlist)\n\n# Permutational invariance (identical particles)\npsi = dicke(N, j, m)  # Dicke states\nJz = jspin(N, 'z')  # Collective operators\n```\n\n**See** `references/advanced.md` for Floquet theory, HEOM, permutational invariance, stochastic solvers, superoperators, and performance optimization.\n\n## Common Workflows\n\n### Simulating a Damped Harmonic Oscillator\n\n```python\n# System parameters\nN = 20  # Hilbert space dimension\nomega = 1.0  # Oscillator frequency\nkappa = 0.1  # Decay rate\n\n# Hamiltonian and collapse operators\nH = omega * num(N)\nc_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * destroy(N)]\n\n# Initial state\npsi0 = coherent(N, 3.0)\n\n# Time evolution\ntlist = np.linspace(0, 50, 200)\nresult = mesolve(H, psi0, tlist, c_ops, e_ops=[num(N)])\n\n# Visualize\nplt.plot(tlist, result.expect[0])\nplt.xlabel('Time')\nplt.ylabel('⟨n⟩')\nplt.title('Photon Number Decay')\nplt.show()\n```\n\n### Two-Qubit Entanglement Dynamics\n\n```python\n# Create Bell state\npsi0 = bell_state('00')\n\n# Local dephasing on each qubit\ngamma = 0.1\nc_ops = [\n    np.sqrt(gamma) * tensor(sigmaz(), qeye(2)),\n    np.sqrt(gamma) * tensor(qeye(2), sigmaz())\n]\n\n# Track entanglement\ndef compute_concurrence(t, psi):\n    rho = ket2dm(psi) if psi.isket else psi\n    return concurrence(rho)\n\ntlist = np.linspace(0, 10, 100)\nresult = mesolve(qeye([2, 2]), psi0, tlist, c_ops)\n\n# Compute concurrence for each state\nC_t = [concurrence(state.proj()) for state in result.states]\n\nplt.plot(tlist, C_t)\nplt.xlabel('Time')\nplt.ylabel('Concurrence')\nplt.title('Entanglement Decay')\nplt.show()\n```\n\n### Jaynes-Cummings Model\n\n```python\n# System parameters\nN = 10  # Cavity Fock space\nwc = 1.0  # Cavity frequency\nwa = 1.0  # Atom frequency\ng = 0.05  # Coupling strength\n\n# Operators\na = tensor(destroy(N), qeye(2))  # Cavity\nsm = tensor(qeye(N), sigmam())  # Atom\n\n# Hamiltonian (RWA)\nH = wc * a.dag() * a + wa * sm.dag() * sm + g * (a.dag() * sm + a * sm.dag())\n\n# Initial state: cavity in coherent state, atom in ground state\npsi0 = tensor(coherent(N, 2), basis(2, 0))\n\n# Dissipation\nkappa = 0.1  # Cavity decay\ngamma = 0.05  # Atomic decay\nc_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a, np.sqrt(gamma) * sm]\n\n# Observables\nn_cav = a.dag() * a\nn_atom = sm.dag() * sm\n\n# Evolve\ntlist = np.linspace(0, 50, 200)\nresult = mesolve(H, psi0, tlist, c_ops, e_ops=[n_cav, n_atom])\n\n# Plot\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(8, 6), sharex=True)\naxes[0].plot(tlist, result.expect[0])\naxes[0].set_ylabel('⟨n_cavity⟩')\naxes[1].plot(tlist, result.expect[1])\naxes[1].set_ylabel('⟨n_atom⟩')\naxes[1].set_xlabel('Time')\nplt.tight_layout()\nplt.show()\n```\n\n## Tips for Efficient Simulations\n\n1. **Truncate Hilbert spaces**: Use smallest dimension that captures dynamics\n2. **Choose appropriate solver**: `sesolve` for pure states is faster than `mesolve`\n3. **Time-dependent terms**: String format (e.g., `'cos(w*t)'`) is fastest\n4. **Store only needed data**: Use `e_ops` instead of storing all states\n5. **Adjust tolerances**: Balance accuracy with computation time via `Options`\n6. **Parallel trajectories**: `mcsolve` automatically uses multiple CPUs\n7. **Check convergence**: Vary `ntraj`, Hilbert space size, and tolerances\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n**Memory issues**: Reduce Hilbert space dimension, use `store_final_state` option, or consider Krylov methods\n\n**Slow simulations**: Use string-based time-dependence, increase tolerances slightly, or try `method='bdf'` for stiff problems\n\n**Numerical instabilities**: Decrease time steps (`nsteps` option), increase tolerances, or check Hamiltonian/operators are properly defined\n\n**Import errors**: Ensure QuTiP is installed correctly; quantum gates require `qutip-qip` package\n\n## References\n\nThis skill includes detailed reference documentation:\n\n- Build operator list: identity for cavity, then identities and Pauli for spins\n    op_list_z = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_p = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_m = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n\n    Jz_list.append(tensor(op_list_z) / 2.0)\n    Jp_list.append(tensor(op_list_p))\n    Jm_list.append(tensor(op_list_m))\n\nJz = sum(Jz_list)\nJp = sum(Jp_list)\nJm = sum(Jm_list)\n\nprint(f\"System size: Hilbert space dimension = {(n_max) * (2**N)}\")\nprint(f\"Collective spin operators constructed\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Dicke Model Hamiltonian\n# ============================================================================\n\n# H = omega_0 * Jz + omega_c * a^dag * a + g * (a^dag + a) * (J+ + J-)\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * n_cav + g * (a_dag + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\nprint(f\"Hamiltonian constructed\")\nprint(f\"Coupling strength g = {g:.4f}\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Collapse Operators for Different Loss Cases\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_collapse_operators(case):\n    \"\"\"\n    Define collapse operators for different loss cases\n\n    Case 1: Local dephasing + local pumping\n    Case 2: Local dephasing + local emission\n    Case 3: Local dephasing + local emission + collective pumping\n    Case 4: Local dephasing + local emission + collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\n    gamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\n    gamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\n    gamma_Up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\n    gamma_Down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity loss (present in all cases)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(kappa) * a)\n\n    # Local dephasing: sum_i sqrt(gamma_phi) * sigma_z^i\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    if case == 1:\n        # Local pumping: sum_i sqrt(gamma_up) * sigma_+^i\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 2:\n        # Local emission: sum_i sqrt(gamma_down) * sigma_-^i\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 3:\n        # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective pumping: sqrt(gamma_Up) * J+\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective emission: sqrt(gamma_Down) * J-\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Steady State Calculation and Wigner Function\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_wigner_for_case(case_num):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate steady state and Wigner function for a given case\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case_num}: Calculating steady state and Wigner function\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = get_collapse_operators(case_num)\n    print(f\"Number of collapse operators: {len(c_ops)}\")\n\n    # Calculate steady state\n    print(\"Solving for steady state...\")\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    print(\"Steady state found\")\n\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom to get cavity state\n    # rho_ss is in the Hilbert space of (cavity, spin1, spin2, spin3, spin4)\n    # We want to trace out the spin degrees of freedom (all but the first)\n    print(\"Tracing out spin degrees of freedom...\")\n\n    # Reshape and trace out spins\n    # The cavity state is obtained by partial trace over spin states\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n\n    print(f\"Cavity state dimension: {rho_cav.dims}\")\n    print(f\"Trace of cavity state: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Calculate Wigner function\n    print(\"Calculating Wigner function (this may take a minute)...\")\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    print(f\"Wigner function shape: {W.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Wigner function calculated successfully\")\n\n    return W\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Create output directory if it doesn't exist\n    output_dir = \"/root\"\n\n    # Calculate Wigner functions for all 4 cases\n    results = {}\n    for case in range(1, 5):\n        W = calculate_wigner_for_case(case)\n        results[case] = W\n\n    # Save results as CSV files\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"Saving results to CSV files\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n\n    for case in range(1, 5):\n        filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f\"{case}.csv\")\n        W = results[case]\n\n        # Build operator list: identity for cavity, then identities and Pauli for spins\n    op_list_z = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_p = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_m = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n\n    Jz_list.append(tensor(op_list_z) / 2.0)\n    Jp_list.append(tensor(op_list_p))\n    Jm_list.append(tensor(op_list_m))\n\nJz = sum(Jz_list)\nJp = sum(Jp_list)\nJm = sum(Jm_list)\n\nprint(f\"System size: Hilbert space dimension = {(n_max) * (2**N)}\")\nprint(f\"Collective spin operators constructed\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Dicke Model Hamiltonian\n# ============================================================================\n\n# H = omega_0 * Jz + omega_c * a^dag * a + g * (a^dag + a) * (J+ + J-)\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * n_cav + g * (a_dag + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\nprint(f\"Hamiltonian constructed\")\nprint(f\"Coupling strength g = {g:.4f}\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Collapse Operators for Different Loss Cases\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_collapse_operators(case):\n    \"\"\"\n    Define collapse operators for different loss cases\n\n    Case 1: Local dephasing + local pumping\n    Case 2: Local dephasing + local emission\n    Case 3: Local dephasing + local emission + collective pumping\n    Case 4: Local dephasing + local emission + collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\n    gamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\n    gamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\n    gamma_Up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\n    gamma_Down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity loss (present in all cases)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(kappa) * a)\n\n    # Local dephasing: sum_i sqrt(gamma_phi) * sigma_z^i\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    if case == 1:\n        # Local pumping: sum_i sqrt(gamma_up) * sigma_+^i\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 2:\n        # Local emission: sum_i sqrt(gamma_down) * sigma_-^i\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 3:\n        # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective pumping: sqrt(gamma_Up) * J+\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective emission: sqrt(gamma_Down) * J-\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Steady State Calculation and Wigner Function\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_wigner_for_case(case_num):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate steady state and Wigner function for a given case\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case_num}: Calculating steady state and Wigner function\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = get_collapse_operators(case_num)\n    print(f\"Number of collapse operators: {len(c_ops)}\")\n\n    # Calculate steady state\n    print(\"Solving for steady state...\")\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    print(\"Steady state found\")\n\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom to get cavity state\n    # rho_ss is in the Hilbert space of (cavity, spin1, spin2, spin3, spin4)\n    # We want to trace out the spin degrees of freedom (all but the first)\n    print(\"Tracing out spin degrees of freedom...\")\n\n    # Reshape and trace out spins\n    # The cavity state is obtained by partial trace over spin states\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n\n    print(f\"Cavity state dimension: {rho_cav.dims}\")\n    print(f\"Trace of cavity state: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Calculate Wigner function\n    print(\"Calculating Wigner function (this may take a minute)...\")\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    print(f\"Wigner function shape: {W.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Wigner function calculated successfully\")\n\n    return W\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Create output directory if it doesn't exist\n    output_dir = \"/root\"\n\n    # Calculate Wigner functions for all 4 cases\n    results = {}\n    for case in range(1, 5):\n        W = calculate_wigner_for_case(case)\n        results[case] = W\n\n    # Save results as CSV files\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"Saving results to CSV files\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n\n    for case in range(1, 5):\n        filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f\"{case}.csv\")\n        W = results[case]\n\n        # Create DataFrame with x and p as coordinates\n        # Flatten the 2D Wigner function into a format suitable for CSV\n        x_grid, p_grid = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n\n        data = {\n            'x': x_grid.flatten(),\n            'p': p_grid.flatten(),\n            'W': W.flatten()\n        }\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n        df.to_csv(filename, index=False)\n\n        print(f\"Case {case}: Saved to {filename}\")\n        print(f\"  Shape: {W.shape}\")\n        print(f\"  Min value: {W.min():.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Max value: {W.max():.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Mean value: {W.mean():.6f}\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Photon number cutoff\n\n# Wigner function grid parameters\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\nxvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\npvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Quantum Operators\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Build operator list structure for tensor products\n# Hilbert space: cavity (n_max dimension) × spin1 (2) × spin2 (2) × spin3 (2) × spin4 (2)\n\n# Cavity operators\na = tensor(destroy(n_max), *[qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])  # Local dephasing rate\n    gamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\n    gamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\n    gamma_Up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\n    gamma_Down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective pumping: sqrt(gamma_Up) * J+\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [Id_cav] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # ============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\nConstructing quantum operators...\")\n\n# Build operator list structure for tensor products\n# Hilbert space: cavity (n_max dimension) × spin1 (2) × spin2 (2) × spin3 (2) × spin4 (2)\n\n# Cavity operators\na = tensor(destroy(n_max), *[qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\na_dag = a.dag()\nn_cav = a_dag * a\n\n# op_list_p = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_m = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n\n    Jz_list.append(tensor(op_list_z) / 2.0)\n    Jp_list.append(tensor(op_list_p))\n    Jm_list.append(tensor(op_list_m))\n\nJz = sum(Jz_list)\nJp = sum(Jp_list)\nJm = sum(Jm_list)\n\nprint(f\"System Local dephasing + local pumping\n    Case 2: Local dephasing + local emission\n    Case 3: Local dephasing + local emission + collective pumping\n    Case 4: Local dephasing + local emission + collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\n    gamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\n    gamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\n    gamma_Up = 0.1  # Collective sqrt(gamma_phi) * sigma_z^i\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective pumping: sqrt(gamma_Up) * J+\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        # Collective emission: sqrt(gamma_Down) * J-\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Case Descriptions\n#   \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case_num}: {case_descriptions[case_num]}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = print(f\"Error finding steady state: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom to get cavity state\n    print(\"Tracing out spin degrees of freedom...\")\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n\n    print(f\"Cavity state dimension: {rho_cav.dims}\")\n    print(f\"Trace of cavity state (sanity check): {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Check cavity state validity\n    cavity_dim = rho_cav.shape[0]\n    print(f\"Cavity Hilbert space dimension: {cavity_dim}\")\n\n    # Calculate Wigner function\n    print(f\"Computing Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} sum(Jm_list)\n\nprint(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {n_max * (2**N)}\")\n\n# Hamiltonian\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * n_cav + g * (a_dag + a) * (Jp + Jm)\nprint(f\"  Hamiltonian constructed\")\n\n# 1\nrho_init_spins = basis(2, 0) * basis(2, 0).dag()  # Ground state for each spin\nfor _ in range(N-1):\n    rho_init_spins = tensor_prod(rho_init_spins, basis(2, 0) * basis(2, 0).dag())\n\nrho0 = tensor_prod(rho_init_cav, rho_init_spins)\nprint(f\"  Initial state: thermal cavity + spin ground state\")\nprint(f\"  Initial state dimension: {rho0.shape}\")\n\ncase_descriptions = {\n    1: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local pumping (γ_↑=0.1)\",\n    2: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1)\",\n    3: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective pumping (γ_Λ=0.1)\",\n    4: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective emission (γ_Υ=0.1)\"\n}\n\ndef get_collapse_operators(case):\n    \"\"\"Define collapse operators for each case\"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01\n    gamma_up = 0.1\n    gamma_down = 0.1\n    gamma_Up = 0.1\n    gamma_Down = 0.1\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity loss (in all cases)\n j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 2:\n        # Local emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 3:\n        # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# Process all cases\nresults = result.states[-1]\n    print(f\"  Steady state obtained (final evolution time point)\")\n\n    # Trace out spins\n    print(f\"  Tracing out spin degrees of freedom...\", end='', flush=True)\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n    print(f\" done\")\n    print(f\"    Cavity state shape: {rho_cav.shape}\")\n    print(f\"    Trace: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Wigner function\n    print(f\"  Computing Wigner function ({grid_points}×{grid_points})...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    t_wig = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local pumping (γ_↑=0.1)\",\n    2: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1)\",\n    3: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective pumping (γ_Λ=0.1)\",\n    4: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective emission (γ_Υ=0.1)\"\n}\n\ndef get_collapse_operators(case):\n    \"\"\"Define collapse operators for each case\"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01\n    gamma_up = 0.1\n    gamma_down = 0.1\n    gamma_Up = 0.1\n    gamma_Down = 0.1\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity loss (in all cases)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(kappa) * a)\n\n    # Local dephasing (in all cases)\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    if case == 1:\n        # Local pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 2:\n        # Local emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 3:\n        # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# Process all cases\nresults = {}\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case}: {case_descriptions[case]}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n    t_case_start = time.time()\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = get_collapse_operators(case)\n    print(f\"  Number of collapse operators: {len(c_ops)}\")\n\n    # Time evolution to find steady state\n    print(f\"  Evolving to steady state...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # Long time evolution to reach steady state\n    tlist = np.linspace(0, 100, 50)  # 50 time steps up to t=100\n\n    result = mesolve(H, rho0, tlist, c_ops, options={'store_states': True})\n\n    t_ev = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_ev:.2f}s\")\n\n    # Extract steady state from final time\n    rho_ss = result.states[-1]\n    print(f\"  Steady state obtained (final evolution time point)\")\n\n    # Trace out spins\n    print(f\"  Tracing out spin degrees of freedom...\", end='', flush=True)\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n    print(f\" done\")\n    print(f\"    Cavity state shape: {rho_cav.shape}\")\n    print(f\"    Trace: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Wigner function\n    print(f\"  Computing Wigner function ({grid_points}×{grid_points})...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    t_wig = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_wig:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"    Min: {W.min():.4e}, Max: {W.max():.4e}, Mean: {W.mean():.4e}\")\n\n    results[case] = W\n\n    t_case = time.time() - t_case_start\n    print(f\"  Case {case} total time: {t_case:.2f}s\")\n\n# Save results\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SAVING RESULTS\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\noutput_dir = \"/root\"\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    if case not in results:\n        print(f\"Case {case}: Skipped (no data)\")\n        continue\n\n    filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f\"{case}.csv\")\n    W = results[case]\n\n    print(f\"\\nCase {case}: Saving to {filename}\")\n\n    x_grid, p_grid = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n    data = {\n        'x': x_grid.flatten(),\n        'p': p_grid.flatten(),\n        'W': W.flatten()\n    }\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    df.to_csv(filename, index=False)\n\n    file_size_mb = os.path.getsize(filename) / (1024 * 1024)\n    print(f\"  File size: {file_size_mb:.2f} MB\")\n    print(f\"  Points saved: {len(df)}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SIMULATION COMPLETE\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"Output files created: 1.csv, 2.csv, 3.csv, 4.csv\")\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Reduced from 16 for faster computation\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\nxvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\npvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N}, ω₀ = {omega0}, ω_c = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}, κ = {kappa}, n_max = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {n_max * (2**N)}\")\nprint(f\"  Wigner grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points}, range: [{x_min}, {x_max}]×[{p_min}, {p_max}]\")\n\n# Build operators\nprint(f\"\\nConstructing quantum operators...\")\na = tensor(destroy(n_max), *[qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\na_dag = a.dag()\nn_cav = a_dag * a\n\nJz_list = []\nJp_list = []\nJm_list = []\n\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list_z = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_p = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n    op_list_m = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n\n    Jz_list.append(tensor(op_list_z) / 2.0)\n    Jp_list.append(tensor(op_list_p))\n    Jm_list.append(tensor(op_list_m))\n\nJz = sum(Jz_list)\nJp = sum(Jp_list)\nJm = sum(Jm_list)\n\n# Hamiltonian\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * n_cav + g * (a_dag + a) * (Jp + Jm)\nprint(f\"  Operators constructed\")\n\n# Initial state\nrho_init_cav = thermal_dm(n_max, 0.5)  # Lower temperature\nrho_init_spins = basis(2, 0) * basis(2, 0).dag()\nfor _ in range(N-1):\n    rho_init_spins = tensor(rho_init_spins, basis(2, 0) * basis(2, 0).dag())\n\nrho0 = tensor(rho_init_cav, rho_init_spins)\nprint(f\"  Initial state prepared: {rho0.shape}\")\n\ncase_descriptions = {\n    1: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local pumping (γ_↑=0.1)\",\n    2: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1)\",\n    3: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective pumping (γ_Λ=0.1)\",\n    4: \"Local dephasing (γ_φ=0.01) + Local emission (γ_↓=0.1) + Collective emission (γ_Υ=0.1)\"\n}\n\ndef get_collapse_operators(case):\n    \"\"\"Define collapse operators for each case\"\"\"\n    gamma_phi = 0.01\n    gamma_up = 0.1\n    gamma_down = 0.1\n    gamma_Up = 0.1\n    gamma_Down = 0.1\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(kappa) * a)\n\n    # Local dephasing\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmaz() for j in range(N)]\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    if case == 1:\n        # Local pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmap() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 2:\n        # Local emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n\n    elif case == 3:\n        # Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# Solver options - optimized for speed\nopts = Options(\n    nsteps=10000,  # Increase solver steps\n    method='bdf',  # Use BDF for stiff systems\n    atol=1e-8,\n    rtol=1e-6\n)\n\n# Process all cases\nresults = {}\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case}: {case_descriptions[case]}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n    t_case_start = time.time()\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = get_collapse_operators(case)\n    print(f\"  Collapse operators: {len(c_ops)}\")\n\n    # Time evolution - optimized time steps\n    print(f\"  Evolving to steady state...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # Use fewer time points but covering larger time range\n    tlist = np.linspace(0, 50, 20)\n    result = mesolve(H, rho0, tlist, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n    t_ev = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_ev:.2f}s\")\n\n    # Extract steady state\n    rho_ss = result.states[-1]\n    print(f\"  Final evolution time: t = {tlist[-1]}\")\n\n    # Trace out spins\n    print(f\"  Tracing out spins...\", end='', flush=True)\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n    print(f\" done\")\n    print(f\"    Cavity dimension: {rho_cav.shape}, Trace: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Wigner function\n    print(f\"  Computing Wigner function ({grid_points}×{grid_points})...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    t_wig = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_wig:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"    Statistics - Min: {W.min():.4e}, Max: {W.max():.4e}, Mean: {W.mean():.4e}\")\n\n    results[case] = W\n\n    t_case = time.time() - t_case_start\n    print(f\"  Case {case} total: {t_case:.2f}s\")\n\n# Save results\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SAVING CSV FILES\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\noutput_dir = \"/root\"\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    if case not in results:\n        print(f\"Case {case}: Skipped\")\n        continue\n\n    filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f\"{case}.csv\")\n    W = results[case]\n\n    print(f\"\\nCase {case}: Saving to {filename}\")\n\n    x_grid, p_grid = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n    data = {\n        'x': x_grid.flatten(),\n        'p': p_grid.flatten(),\n        'W': W.flatten()\n    }\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    df.to_csv(filename, index=False)\n\n    size_mb = os.path.getsize(filename) / (1024 * 1024)\n    print(f\"  File: {size_mb:.2f} MB, Points: {len(df)}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SIMULATION COMPLETE\")\nprint(f\"Files: 1.csv, 2.csv, 3.csv, 4.csv\")\nprint(f\"Note: Simulation used n_max={n_max} for practical computation time\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":168,"cache_read_input_tokens":53846,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":168,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0e1a14d0-e569-4517-abea-7bca11ba009e","uuid":"85946bf6-9277-44f0-a1ba-617e414dc546"}

Local emission + collective pumping\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Up) * Jp)\n\n    elif case == 4:\n        # Local emission + collective emission\n        for i in range(N):\n            op_list = [qeye(n_max)] + [qeye(2) if j != i else sigmam() for j in range(N)]\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(op_list))\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_Down) * Jm)\n\n    return c_ops\n\n# Solver options - optimized for speed\nopts = Options(\n    nsteps=10000,  # Increase solver steps\n    method='bdf',  # Use BDF for stiff systems\n    atol=1e-8,\n    rtol=1e-6\n)\n\n# Process all cases\nresults = {}\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"Case {case}: {case_descriptions[case]}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n    t_case_start = time.time()\n\n    # Get collapse operators\n    c_ops = get_collapse_operators(case)\n    print(f\"  Collapse operators: {len(c_ops)}\")\n\n    # Time evolution - optimized time steps\n    print(f\"  Evolving to steady state...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # Use fewer time points but covering larger time range\n    tlist = np.linspace(0, 50, 20)\n    result = mesolve(H, rho0, tlist, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n    t_ev = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_ev:.2f}s\")\n\n    # Extract steady state\n    rho_ss = result.states[-1]\n    print(f\"  Final evolution time: t = {tlist[-1]}\")\n\n    # Trace out spins\n    print(f\"  Tracing out spins...\", end='', flush=True)\n    rho_cav = rho_ss.ptrace(0)\n    print(f\" done\")\n    print(f\"    Cavity dimension: {rho_cav.shape}, Trace: {rho_cav.tr():.6f}\")\n\n    # Wigner function\n    print(f\"  Computing Wigner function ({grid_points}×{grid_points})...\", end='', flush=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n    W = wigner(rho_cav, xvec, pvec)\n    t_wig = time.time() - t_start\n    print(f\" done in {t_wig:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"    Statistics - Min: {W.min():.4e}, Max: {W.max():.4e}, Mean: {W.mean():.4e}\")\n\n    results[case] = W\n\n    t_case = time.time() - t_case_start\n    print(f\"  Case {case} total: {t_case:.2f}s\")\n\n# Save results\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SAVING CSV FILES\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\noutput_dir = \"/root\"\n\nfor case in range(1, 5):\n    if case not in results:\n        print(f\"Case {case}: Skipped\")\n        continue\n\n    filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f\"{case}.csv\")\n    W = results[case]\n\n    print(f\"\\nCase {case}: Saving to {filename}\")\n\n    x_grid, p_grid = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n    data = {\n        'x': x_grid.flatten(),\n        'p': p_grid.flatten(),\n        'W': W.flatten()\n    }\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    df.to_csv(filename, index=False)\n\n    size_mb = os.path.getsize(filename) / (1024 * 1024)\n    print(f\"  File: {size_mb:.2f} MB, Points: {len(df)}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\nprint(\"SIMULATION COMPLETE\")\nprint(f\"Files: 1.csv, 2.csv, 3.csv, 4.csv\")\nprint(f\"Note: Simulation used n_max={n_max} for practical computation time\")\nprint(f\"{'='*80}\")\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
